than the largest present; validates code, category, amount, date (exit 3) |\n| TXN-002 | Get a transaction with `ledger txn get` | Writes row; absent id exits 4 |\n| TXN-003 | Amend a transaction absent id exits 4; negative balance rejected with exit 3 |\n\n### Feature: Transfers\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| XFER-001 | Transfer between accounts with `ledger transfer` | Writes two `TRANSFER` rows with ids N and N+1; atomic; rejected if would leave negative balance |\n\n### Feature: Command Surface and Reinitialization\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Usage errors exit 2 | Unknown command or wrong argument count exits 2 with diagnostic on stderr |\n| CLI-002 | Re-initialization drops every row | Second `init` leaves output and exits zero |\n| AC-002 | LOAD-004 | A rejected transaction load writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error |\n| AC-003 | TXN-001 | A posted transaction with an account with transactions exits 3 and leaves the account in place |\n| AC-006 | XFER-001 | A transfer whose two accounts are equal exits 3 |\n| AC-007 | XFER-001 | A transfer with a non-positive amount exits 3 |\n| AC-008 | TXN-001 | A transaction whose category is not in the categories table exits 3 |\n| AC-009 | ACCT-001 | An account code longer than 12 characters, or containing characters outside `A-Z` and `0-9`, exits 3 |\n| AC-010 | ACCT-001 | An account name longer than 40 characters, or containing a comma or newline, exits 3 |\n| AC-011 | TXN-001 | A memo longer than 60 characters, or containing a comma or newline, exits 3 |\n| AC-012 | REPORT-002 | Categories with no transactions appear in the categories report with zero counts and zero amounts |\n| AC-013 | REPORT-003 | Months with no transactions are omitted from the monthly report |\n| AC-014 | TXN-003 | Amending a transaction to an amount that would leave a balance negative exits 3 and changes nothing |\n\n## system is a Python 3.11+ command-line executable named `ledger` at the application root, backed by SQLite (or PostgreSQL when `DATABASE_URL` is a PostgreSQL DSN) through the shipped persistence library. Inputs are CLI arguments and CSV files. Outputs are CSV on standard output and diagnostics on standard error. Exit codes are 0, 1, 2, 3, and 4 per the contract. Execution flow: `init` creates schema; `load` populates seed data; commands mutate tables under atomic, balance-checked writes; reports are computed from the database at query time.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- SCHEMA-001, SCHEMA-002: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Commands table; `sources/run.sh` A1, A2, Z1, Z2. Requires the schema capability and persistence library.\n- LOAD-001..005: `sources/run.sh` B1..B4, D1..D3; fixtures `sources/seed_*.csv`. Requires load capability and atomic write transaction.\n- REPORT-001..004: `sources/run.sh` C1..C3, H1..H3, U2; `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Output section. Requires computed reports, no embedded answers.\n- ACCT-001..004: `sources/run.sh` E1..E8. Requires account CRUD with in-use guard.\n- TXN-001..004: `sources/run.sh` F1..F10, G3..G5. Requires transaction CRUD with rule checks.\n- XFER-001: `sources/run.sh` G1, G2. Requires atomic two-row write.\n- CLI-001, CLI-002: `sources/run.sh` U1, Z1, Z2. Requires argument dispatch and usage-error exit code 2.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\nStory-scoped tests run bounded slices of the application's own `bin/test.sh` where the story owns them. The terminal verification story runs the full supplied grader `sh sources/run.sh` and the full application `bin/test.sh`. Sea Trial st-001 is the sole acceptance verdict for the grader. No story runs the entire grader as a unit check.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\nBuild order: schema (SCHEMA-001) → counting (SCHEMA-002) → loads (LOAD-001..005) → account CRUD (ACCT-001..004) → transaction CRUD (TXN-001..004) → transfers (XFER-001) → reports (REPORT-001..004) → usage and reinitialization (CLI-001..002) → terminal full verification. Accounts and categories load before transactions. Balance-rule enforcement must exist before any mutating command is verified against negative-balance checks.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\n- No cross-source conflicts found. INSTRUCTIONS.md is the single definition for commands, output, and exit codes; run.sh is consistent with it.\n- The Rigging catalog has no persistence-library component that matches `DATABASE.md`-generated access by name; the stack is recorded in TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md with conventional choices and does not block planning.\n- Load rules for a non-empty target table are not stated in the sources. Per the narrowest consistent reading, `load` on a non-empty table is treated as an operation that adds rows only when every row passes validation and no key collides; a key collision is rejected with exit 3 (LOAD-005). BEGIN ARTIFACT ANALYSIS.md ===\n# Blueprint Analysis: drydock Layers Testing Uat Case\n\n## Commander Expectations\n\n- assert the product is a command-line cash ledger executable named `ledger` at the application root\n- assert every command either completes in full or changes nothing\n- assert all data is stored through the shipped persistence library, selected by `DATABASE_URL`\n- assert every report is computed from the database and no report answer is embedded in application code\n- assert the supplied grader `sources/run.sh` exits zero against the completed application\n\n## Crew\n\n| Crew | Charge |\n|---|---|\n| Commander | Defines intent and decides what done means. |\n| Team Lead | Confirms epic completeness and stakeholder expectations. |\n| Planning Crew | Authors atomic specifications and the ordered Manifest. |\n| Shipyard Crew | Builds the tickets without synchronous Commander access. |\n\n## Story List\n\nfeatures: 6\nstories: 22\n\n### Feature: Schema and Bulk Load\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| SCHEMA-001 | Initialize the three-table schema with `ledger init` | `init` drops and recreates accounts, categories, and transactions empty; `count` reports zero rows each |\n| SCHEMA-002 | Count rows per table with `ledger count` | Outputs `table,rows` header then accounts, categories, transactions rows in that order |\n| LOAD-001 | Load accounts from CSV with `ledger load accounts` | Loads 4 seed accounts; writes `table,loaded` and `accounts,4` |\n| LOAD-002 | Load categories from CSV with `ledger load categories` | Loads 7 seed categories; writes `table,loaded` and `categories,7` |\n| LOAD-003 | Load transactions from CSV with `ledger load transactions` | Loads 120 seed transactions; writes `table,loaded` and `transactions,120` |\n| LOAD-004 | Reject a load containing an invalid row atomically | Load of `seed_transactions_bad.csv` exits 3 and stores none of rows 1 to 10; row 11 unknown account rejected |\n| LOAD-005 | Reject a load whose target table is already non-empty or duplicate-keyed | Duplicate key on accounts load exits 3 and leaves tables unchanged |\n\n### Feature: Reporting\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| REPORT-001 | Balances report by account | `account,name,transactions,balance` per account by code, then `TOTAL` row |\n| REPORT-002 | Categories report with inflow, outflow, and net | Per category by code including empty categories, then `TOTAL` row |\n| REPORT-003 | Monthly report by `YYYY-MM` | Per month with transactions ascending, then `TOTAL` row |\n| REPORT-004 | Reject unknown report name with exit 2 | `report nosuch` exits 2 |\n\n### Feature: Account Management\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| ACCT-001 | Add an account with `ledger account add` | Writes the new account row; duplicate code exits 3 |\n| ACCT-002 | Get an account with `ledger account get` | Writes account row; absent code exits 4 |\n| ACCT-003 | Rename an account with `ledger account rename` | Writes updated row; absent code exits 4 |\n| ACCT-004 | Delete an account with `ledger account delete` | Writes `deleted` and code; refused with exit 3 while transactions exist; absent exits 4 |\n\n### Feature: Transaction Management\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TXN-001 | Post a transaction with `ledger txn post` | Writes the new row with id one more than the largest present; validates code, category, amount, date (exit 3) |\n| TXN-002 | Get a transaction with `ledger txn get` | Writes row; absent id exits 4 |\n| TXN-003 | Amend a transaction amount with `ledger txn amend` | Only amount changes; absent id exits 4; negative balance rejected with exit 3 |\n| TXN-004 | Delete a transaction with `ledger txn delete` | Writes `deleted` and id; absent id exits 4; negative balance rejected with exit 3 |\n\n### Feature: Transfers\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| XFER-001 | Transfer between accounts with `ledger transfer` | Writes two `TRANSFER` rows with ids N and N+1; atomic; rejected if would leave negative balance |\n\n### Feature: Command Surface and Reinitialization\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Usage errors exit 2 | Unknown command or wrong argument count exits 2 with diagnostic on stderr |\n| CLI-002 | Re-initialization drops every row | Second `init` leaves accounts, categories, transactions at zero rows |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | SCHEMA-001 | `ledger init` writes nothing to standard output and exits zero |\n| AC-002 | LOAD-004 | A rejected transaction load writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error |\n| AC-003 | TXN-001 | A posted transaction with a fractional-cent amount (for example `1.234`) exits 3 |\n| AC-004 | TXN-001 | A posted transaction with an invalid calendar date (for example `2026-02-30`) exits 3 |\n| AC-005 | ACCT-004 | Deleting an account with transactions exits 3 and leaves the account in place |\n| AC-006 | XFER-001 | A transfer whose two accounts are equal exits 3 |\n| AC-007 | XFER-001 | A transfer with a non-positive amount exits 3 |\n| AC-008 | TXN-001 | A transaction whose category is not in the categories table exits 3 |\n| AC-009 | ACCT-001 | An account code longer than 12 characters, or containing characters outside `A-Z` and `0-9`, exits 3 |\n| AC-010 | ACCT-001 | An account name longer than 40 characters, or containing a comma or newline, exits 3 |\n| AC-011 | TXN-001 | A memo longer than 60 characters, or containing a comma or newline, exits 3 |\n| AC-012 | REPORT-002 | Categories with no transactions appear in the categories report with zero counts and zero amounts |\n| AC-013 | REPORT-003 | Months with no transactions are omitted from the monthly report |\n| AC-014 | TXN-003 | Amending a transaction to an amount that would leave a balance negative exits 3 and changes nothing |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery implication |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `sources/run.sh` | instruction-to-test | `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` | run.sh cites the Command table, exit codes, and output formats defined in INSTRUCTIONS.md | Build must satisfy the INSTRUCTIONS.md contract exactly; run.sh is the acceptance verdict |\n| `sources/seed_accounts.csv` | dependency | LOAD-001, REPORT-001 | Loaded by `run.sh` B1; balances reported by account | Load before reporting |\n| `sources/seed_categories.csv` | dependency | LOAD-002, REPORT-002 | Loaded by `run.sh` B2; referenced by transactions | Categories must load before transactions |\n| `sources/seed_transactions.csv` | dependency | LOAD-003, REPORT-001..003 | Loaded by `run.sh` B3; drives all known-answer reports | Accounts and categories must exist first |\n| `sources/seed_transactions_bad.csv` | dependency | LOAD-004 | Used by `run.sh` D1; row 11 references unknown account `NOSUCH` | Atomic rejection required |\n| `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` | dependency | `sources/run.sh` | Scoring entry point is supplied; known answers hardcoded in run.sh | Application must not read run.sh |\n\n## Source Roles\n\n| Path | Role | Plan disposition | Build disposition |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md | normative specification | context | prompt-only |\n| sources/run.sh | conformance harness | compass | stage |\n| sources/seed_accounts.csv | fixture | context | stage |\n| sources/seed_categories.csv | fixture | context | stage |\n| sources/seed_transactions.csv | fixture | context | stage |\n| sources/seed_transactions_bad.csv | fixture | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\nThe system is a Python 3.11+ command-line executable named `ledger` at the application root, backed by SQLite (or PostgreSQL when `DATABASE_URL` is a PostgreSQL DSN) through the shipped persistence library. Inputs are CLI arguments and CSV files. Outputs are CSV on standard output and diagnostics on standard error. Exit codes are 0, 1, 2, 3, and 4 per the contract. Execution flow: `init` creates schema; `load` populates seed data; commands mutate tables under atomic, balance-checked writes; reports are computed from the database at query time.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- SCHEMA-001, SCHEMA-002: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Commands table; `sources/run.sh` A1, A2, Z1, Z2. Requires the schema capability and persistence library.\n- LOAD-001..005: `sources/run.sh` B1..B4, D1..D3; fixtures `sources/seed_*.csv`. Requires load capability and atomic write transaction.\n- REPORT-001..004: `sources/run.sh` C1..C3, H1..H3, U2; `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Output section. Requires computed reports, no embedded answers.\n- ACCT-001..004: `sources/run.sh` E1..E8. Requires account CRUD with in-use guard.\n- TXN-001..004: `sources/run.sh` F1..F10, G3..G5. Requires transaction CRUD with rule checks.\n- XFER-001: `sources/run.sh` G1, G2. Requires atomic two-row write.\n- CLI-001, CLI-002: `sources/run.sh` U1, Z1, Z2. Requires argument dispatch and usage-error exit code 2.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\nStory-scoped tests run bounded slices of the application's own `bin/test.sh` where the story owns them. The terminal verification story runs the full supplied grader `sh sources/run.sh` and the full application `bin/test.sh`. Sea Trial st-001 is the sole acceptance verdict for the grader. No story runs the entire grader as a unit check.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\nBuild order: schema (SCHEMA-001) → counting (SCHEMA-002) → loads (LOAD-001..005) → account CRUD (ACCT-001..004) → transaction CRUD (TXN-001..004) → transfers (XFER-001) → reports (REPORT-001..004) → usage and reinitialization (CLI-001..002) → terminal full verification. Accounts and categories load before transactions. Balance-rule enforcement must exist before any mutating command is verified against negative-balance checks.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\n- No cross-source conflicts found. INSTRUCTIONS.md is the single definition for commands, output, and exit codes; run.sh is consistent with it.\n- The Rigging catalog has no persistence-library component that matches `DATABASE.md`-generated access by name; the stack is recorded in TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md with conventional choices and does not block planning.\n- Load rules for a non-empty target table are not stated in the sources. Per the narrowest consistent reading, `load` on a non-empty table is treated as an operation that adds rows only when every row passes validation and no key collides; a key collision is rejected with exit 3 (LOAD-005). This is recorded as an analysis choice, not a Commander decision.\n\n## Analysis Notes\n\ngenerated: 2026-10-20\nblueprint: stack: not declared\n  display_name: not proposed\n  short_description: not proposed\n\nUnattended UAT policy applied: ambiguous load-into-non-empty behavior resolved to the narrowest consistent
